##### Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: household.widowed_this_year
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Your spouse died in 2025 and you didn't remarry in 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.spouse_died_this_year, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.married_dec31, {const: false}]}
    note: "The source does not define this interface. It is read from i1040gi Married Filing Jointly ('Your spouse died in 2025 and you didn't remarry in 2025') and Pub. 501 'Spouse died during the year'. A spouse who died in 2025 followed by remarriage before year end is covered by married_dec31, not by this line."

  - id: household.married
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Considered married for the whole year for filing status purposes"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.married_dec31, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.widowed_this_year, {const: true}]}
    note: "Open point: whether 'married' means only married at the end of 2025. Chosen reading: Pub. 501 Marital Status, which says that if your spouse died during the year you are considered married for the whole year for filing status purposes. So this line is married at December 31 (including married but considered unmarried for head of household) or widowed in 2025 without remarrying."

  - id: household.h_hoh_test1_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of dependents meeting head of household Test 1 (parent whose main home you kept up)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_test1, {const: true}]}

  - id: household.h_hoh_test2_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons meeting head of household Test 2 (qualifying person who lived with you more than half the year)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_test2, {const: true}]}

  - id: household.h_unmarried_for_hoh
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You are unmarried (or considered unmarried) for head of household"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.considered_unmarried, {const: true}]}

  - id: household.h_hoh_qualifies
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the Head of household box only if you are unmarried (or considered unmarried) and either Test 1 or Test 2 applies"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.h_unmarried_for_hoh, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, gt: [household.h_hoh_test1_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [household.home_cost, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, gt: [household.h_hoh_test2_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
    note: "Test 2 says 'You paid over half the cost of keeping up a home in which you lived'. The per-person dependent.hoh_test2 is taken to cover only the person's own conditions, so the cost condition is tested here through household.home_cost. Test 1's cost condition is per parent and is taken to be inside dependent.hoh_test1."

  - id: household.hoh_fails
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household is checked but the head of household requirements are not met"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.h_hoh_qualifies, {const: false}]}
    note: "Open point: whether this interface is limited to returns claiming head of household. Chosen reading: it is true only when the Filing Status is hoh and the i1040gi Head of Household conditions are not met (unmarried or considered unmarried, and Test 1 or Test 2). On any other status it is false."

  - id: household.cp_marriage_ended
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married during 2025 while living in a community property state, and the marriage ended before the end of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.community_property, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.married_dec31, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.cp_married, {const: true}]}
    note: "Grounded in Pub. 501: 'If you were considered married for part of the year and lived in a community property state ... special rules may apply ... See Pub. 555'. The conditions follow when cp_married is asked: you lived in a community property state, are not filing jointly, and were not married at year end."

  - id: household.h_taxpayers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of person entries for the taxpayer"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "taxpayer"}]}

  - id: household.h_spouses
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of person entries for the spouse"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "spouse"}]}

  - id: household.roster_bad
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The persons entered on the return do not match the filing status"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, ne: [household.h_taxpayers, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, ne: [household.h_spouses, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, ne: [household.h_spouses, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
    note: "The IRS pages do not define this interface. Chosen reading from i1040gi: a joint return reports the combined income of both spouses, and on a separate return you report only your own income. So the roster needs exactly one taxpayer entry, plus exactly one spouse entry on a joint return and none otherwise."

  - id: household.filing_status_conflict
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The filing status checked is not one the marital facts allow"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "single"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.h_unmarried_for_hoh, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "qss"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.qss_eligible, {const: false}]}
    note: "Chosen reading from i1040gi Filing Status and Pub. 501 Marital Status. Single requires being unmarried at December 31 and not widowed in 2025. Married filing jointly and married filing separately require being considered married, which includes a spouse who died in 2025 when you did not remarry. Head of household requires unmarried or considered unmarried; its other tests are covered by household.hoh_fails. Qualifying surviving spouse requires all five tests (household.qss_eligible). A registered domestic partnership is not a marriage, so household.rdp does not enter this line."

Second transcription's note: Open point: whether this interface is limited to returns claiming head of household. Chosen reading: it is true only when the Filing Status is hoh and the i1040gi Head of Household conditions are not met (unmarried or considered unmarried, and Test 1 or Test 2). On any other status it is false.

Ruling: open
Reviewed in: pass 9b2e82e812c5

Second transcription's note: The source does not define this interface. It is read from i1040gi Married Filing Jointly ('Your spouse died in 2025 and you didn't remarry in 2025') and Pub. 501 'Spouse died during the year'. A spouse who died in 2025 followed by remarriage before year end is covered by married_dec31, not by this line.

Ruling: open
Reviewed in: pass 9b2e82e812c5

Second transcription's note: Chosen reading from i1040gi Filing Status and Pub. 501 Marital Status. Single requires being unmarried at December 31 and not widowed in 2025. Married filing jointly and married filing separately require being considered married, which includes a spouse who died in 2025 when you did not remarry. Head of household requires unmarried or considered unmarried; its other tests are covered by household.hoh_fails. Qualifying surviving spouse requires all five tests (household.qss_eligible). A registered domestic partnership is not a marriage, so household.rdp does not enter this line.
